1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on genetics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 134. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 250 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on genetics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 134. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 250. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any genetics module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 656. The projective resolution has length at most 195 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the genetics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^656 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 195-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
